function safeString(value, maxLength = 500) { if (value === null || value === undefined) return ""; if (!["string", "number", "boolean"].includes(typeof value)) return ""; return String(value).replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g, "").trim().slice(0, maxLength); } function corsHeaders(request, env) { const origin = request.headers.get("Origin") || ""; const configured = String(env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN || "").trim().replace(/\/$/, ""); const requestOrigin = new URL(request.url).origin; const headers = { "Vary": "Origin", "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, PATCH, OPTIONS", "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, Authorization", "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400" }; if (origin && (configured ? origin === configured : origin === requestOrigin)) headers["Access-Control-Allow-Origin"] = origin; return headers; } function json(request, env, payload, status = 200) { return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload), { status, headers: { ...corsHeaders(request, env), "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", "Cache-Control": "no-store", "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff", "Referrer-Policy": "strict-origin-when-cross-origin", "X-Frame-Options": "DENY", "Permissions-Policy": "camera=(), geolocation=()", "Strict-Transport-Security": "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains" } }); } async function readJsonLimited(request, maxBytes) { const declared = Number(request.headers.get("Content-Length") || 0); if (declared > maxBytes) return { error: "too_large" }; const reader = request.body?.getReader(); if (!reader) return { value: {} }; const chunks = []; let total = 0; while (true) { const part = await reader.read(); if (part.done) break; total += part.value.byteLength; if (total > maxBytes) { try { await reader.cancel(); } catch (_) {} return { error: "too_large" }; } chunks.push(part.value); } const bytes = new Uint8Array(total); let offset = 0; for (const chunk of chunks) { bytes.set(chunk, offset); offset += chunk.byteLength; } try { return { value: JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) }; } catch (_) { return { error: "invalid" }; } } function makeReference() { const d = new Date(); const date = `${d.getUTCFullYear()}${String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`; return `MPX-REQ-${date}-${crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll("-", "").slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`; } async function notifyOwner(env, requestData) { if (!env.RESEND_API_KEY || !env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || !env.NOTIFY_EMAIL) return false; try { const safeFields = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(requestData.fields || {}).map(([k, v]) => [safeString(k, 60), safeString(v, 1000)])); const response = await fetch("https://api.resend.com/emails", { method: "POST", headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${env.RESEND_API_KEY}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ from: env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL, to: [env.NOTIFY_EMAIL], subject: `Marrakechpixim â€” new ${safeString(requestData.kind, 40)} request ${safeString(requestData.reference, 80)}`, text: `A new request was saved in Cloudflare D1.\ Reference: ${safeString(requestData.reference, 80)}\ Type: ${safeString(requestData.kind, 40)}\ Created at: ${safeString(requestData.createdAt, 60)}\ \ Submitted fields:\ ${Object.entries(safeFields).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join("\ ")}\ \ This email is a notification only. The protected D1 record is authoritative.` }) }); return response.ok; } catch (_) { return false; } } function resolvePayPalProduct(body) { const productId = safeString(body?.productId, 80); const product = PAYPAL_PRODUCTS[productId]; if (!product) return null; if (productId === "project-support-donation") { const amount = safeString(body?.donationAmount, 16); if (!product.allowedAmounts.includes(amount)) return null; return { id: product.id, name: product.name, description: product.description, amount, currency: product.currency }; } return product; } function paypalApiBase(env) { return String(env.PAYPAL_MODE || "sandbox").toLowerCase() === "live" ? "https://api-m.paypal.com" : "https://api-m.sandbox.paypal.com"; } function paymentOriginAllowed(request, env) { const origin = request.headers.get("Origin") || ""; if (!origin) return false; const allowed = String(env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN || "").replace(/\/$/, ""); const requestOrigin = new URL(request.url).origin; // Do not accept a wildcard origin for checkout. Prefer an exact configured origin. return allowed ? (allowed !== "*" && origin === allowed) : origin === requestOrigin; } async function paypalAccessToken(env) { if (!env.PAYPAL_CLIENT_ID || !env.PAYPAL_CLIENT_SECRET) throw new Error("PayPal credentials are not configured."); const auth = btoa(`${env.PAYPAL_CLIENT_ID}:${env.PAYPAL_CLIENT_SECRET}`); const response = await fetch(`${paypalApiBase(env)}/v1/oauth2/token`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Authorization": `Basic ${auth}`, "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" }, body: "grant_type=client_credentials" }); const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!response.ok || !data.access_token) throw new Error("PayPal authentication failed."); return data.access_token; } async function paypalApi(env, token, path, options = {}) { const response = await fetch(`${paypalApiBase(env)}${path}`, { ...options, headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json", "Accept": "application/json", ...(options.headers || {}) } }); const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!response.ok) { const err = new Error("PayPal checkout request failed."); err.status = response.status; err.paypalIssue = safeString(data.name || data.details?.[0]?.issue || "PAYPAL_API_ERROR", 80); throw err; } return data; } async function sha256Hex(value) { const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(value); const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", bytes); return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(""); } function makePaymentReference() { const d = new Date(); const date = `${d.getUTCFullYear()}${String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}${String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`; return `MPX-PAY-${date}-${crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll("-", "").slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`; } async function notifyPayment(env, payment) { if (!env.RESEND_API_KEY || !env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || !env.NOTIFY_EMAIL) return false; try { const response = await fetch("https://api.resend.com/emails", { method: "POST", headers: { "Authorization": `Bearer ${env.RESEND_API_KEY}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ from: env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL, to: [env.NOTIFY_EMAIL], subject: `Marrakechpixim â€” payment confirmed ${payment.reference}`, text: `A PayPal payment was verified by the server.\nReference: ${payment.reference}\nPurpose: ${payment.productName || payment.productId}\nAmount: ${payment.amount} ${payment.currency}\nStatus: COMPLETED\nPayPal order ID: ${payment.orderId}\nCapture ID: ${payment.captureId}\nPaid at: ${payment.paidAt}\nMode: ${payment.mode}\n\nThis email is a notification only. The authoritative record is in the protected Cloudflare D1 payment log at /admin/payments.` }) }); return response.ok; } catch (_) { return false; } } function paymentAdminPage() { return `Marrakechpixim â€” Payment log

Open checkout from the official Marrakechpixim website." }, 403); if (String(env.PAYPAL_ENABLED || "false").toLowerCase() !== "true") return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "PayPal checkout is not enabled by the administrator yet." }, 503); if (!env.DB || !env.PAYPAL_CLIENT_ID || !env.PAYPAL_CLIENT_SECRET || !String(env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN || "").trim() || String(env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN).trim() === "*") return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "PayPal credentials, payment storage, or exact allowed website origin are not configured." }, 503); const length = Number(request.headers.get("Content-Length") || 0); if (length > 3000) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Request is too large." }, 413); const parsedBody = await readJsonLimited(request, 3000); if (parsedBody.error) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? "Request is too large." : "Invalid checkout request." }, parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? 413 : 400); const body = parsedBody.value; const product = resolvePayPalProduct(body); if (!product) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Unknown payment purpose or unsupported amount. AND created_at > ?").bind(ipHash, since).first(); if (Number(recent?.total || 0) >= 8) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Too many checkout attempts. Please wait 10 minutes and try again." }, 429); const paymentId = crypto.randomUUID(); const reference = makePaymentReference(); const createdAt = new Date().toISOString(); const mode = String(env.PAYPAL_MODE || "sandbox").toLowerCase() === "live" ? No payment was taken." }, 500); try { const token = await paypalAccessToken(env); const order = await paypalApi(env, token, "/v2/checkout/orders", { method: "POST", headers: { "PayPal-Request-Id": crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll("-", "").slice(0, 32) }, body: JSON.stringify({ intent: "CAPTURE", purchase_units: [{ reference_id: product.id, description: product.description, custom_id: paymentId, invoice_id: reference, amount: { currency_code: product.currency, value: product.amount } }], application_context: { brand_name: "Marrakechpixim", user_action: "PAY_NOW", shipping_preference: "NO_SHIPPING", return_url: `${new URL(request.url).origin}/#mode-de-paiement`, cancel_url: `${new URL(request.url).origin}/#mode-de-paiement` } }) }); if (!order.id || !Array.isArray(order.links)) throw new Error("PayPal did not return a checkout order."); const savedOrder = await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE payments SET paypal_order_id = ?, status = 'created', updated_at = ? AND status = 'creating'") .bind(order.id, new Date().toISOString(), paymentId).run(); if (!savedOrder.success) throw new Error("Could not save the PayPal order in the payment database."); const verifiedOrder = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT paypal_order_id, status FROM payments WHERE payment_id = ?").bind(paymentId).first(); if (verifiedOrder?.paypal_order_id !== order.id || verifiedOrder?.status !== "created") throw new Error("The PayPal order could not be reconciled with the payment record."); return json(request, env, { success: true, orderId: order.id, reference, status: "created" }, 201); } catch (error) { await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE payments SET status = 'failed', error_code = ?, updated_at = ? No successful payment has been recorded. Please try again later." }, 502); } } if (url.pathname === "/api/paypal/cancel-order" && request.method === "POST") { if (!paymentOriginAllowed(request, env)) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Origin not allowed." }, 403); if (String(env.PAYPAL_ENABLED || "false").toLowerCase() !== "true" || !env.DB || !String(env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN || "").trim() || String(env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN).trim() === "*") return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "PayPal checkout is not configured." }, 503); const parsedBody = await readJsonLimited(request, 1000); if (parsedBody.error) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? LIMIT 1").bind(orderId).first(); if (afterCancel?.status === "paid") return json(request, env, { success: true, cancelled: false, paid: true, message: "This order was verified as paid before cancellation could be recorded." }); if (afterCancel?.status !== "cancelled") return json(request, env, { success: false, cancelled: false, paid: false, message: "The cancellation status could not be confirmed. Manual review is required." }, 409); const paidAt = capture.create_time || new Date().toISOString(); const captureId = safeString(capture.id, 100); const update = await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE payments SET status = 'paid', paypal_capture_id = ?, paid_at = ?, updated_at = ?, error_code = NULL WHERE payment_id = ? WHERE payment_id = ?") .bind(safeString(error.paypalIssue || "CAPTURE_VERIFICATION_FAILED", 80), new Date().toISOString(), payment.payment_id).run(); return json(request, env, { success: false, paid: false, message: "The server could not verify the PayPal capture. No success confirmation was issued. If PayPal charged you, do not pay again; contact support with your order ID." }, 502); } } // Public discovery through Wikidata. This is not a complete company registry and is independent of payment processing. if (url.pathname === "/api/company-search" && request.method === "GET") { const q = safeString(url.searchParams.get("q"), 160).normalize("NFC"); if (q.length < 2) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Enter at least 2 characters to search." }, 400); // Bound upstream latency so a slow third-party API cannot hold the Worker request indefinitely. const fetchWikidataJson = async (target, timeoutMs = 6500) => { const controller = new AbortController(); const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort("Wikidata request timed out"), timeoutMs); try { const response = await fetch(target, { signal: controller.signal, headers: { "Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": "Marrakechpixim/1.0 (mailto:Marrakechpixim@gmail.com)" } }); if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Wikidata HTTP " + response.status); const data = await response.json(); if (!data || typeof data !== "object") throw new Error("Invalid Wikidata response"); return data; } finally { clearTimeout(timer); } }; try { const languageInput = safeString(url.searchParams.get("lang"), 8).toLowerCase(); const allowedLanguages = new Set(["en", "fr", "es", "ar", "pt", "ru", "zh"]); const language = allowedLanguages.has(languageInput) ? languageInput : "en"; const apiUrl = new URL("https://www.wikidata.org/w/api.php"); apiUrl.search = new URLSearchParams({ action: "wbsearchentities", search: q, language, uselang: language, format: "json", limit: "8", type: "item", maxlag: "5", origin: "*" }).toString(); const searchData = await fetchWikidataJson(apiUrl.toString()); const entities = (Array.isArray(searchData.search) ? searchData.search : []) .filter(x => x && /^Q\d+$/.test(String(x.id || ""))) .slice(0, 8); let entityData = {}; let detailsAvailable = true; if (entities.length) { const detailUrl = new URL("https://www.wikidata.org/w/api.php"); detailUrl.search = new URLSearchParams({ action: "wbgetentities", ids: entities.map(x => x.id).join("|"), props: "claims", format: "json", maxlag: "5", origin: "*" }).toString(); try { const details = await fetchWikidataJson(detailUrl.toString(), 4500); entityData = details.entities && typeof details.entities === "object" ? details.entities : {}; } catch (_) { // Search labels still work if the optional website-detail lookup is unavailable. detailsAvailable = false; } } const results = entities.map(x => { const claims = entityData[x.id]?.claims || {}; const websiteClaim = (Array.isArray(claims.P856) ? claims.P856 : []).find(c => c?.mainsnak?.datavalue?.value); let website = websiteClaim ? String(websiteClaim.mainsnak.datavalue.value).trim() : ""; if (website && !/^https?:\/\//i.test(website)) website = "https://" + website; try { const parsedWebsite = new URL(website); if (!/^https?:$/.test(parsedWebsite.protocol)) website = ""; } catch (_) { website = ""; } return { id: x.id, label: safeString(x.label, 250), description: safeString(x.description, 600), entityUrl: "https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/" + x.id, website }; }); return json(request, env, { success: true, provider: "Wikidata", query: q, language, results, detailsAvailable, note: "Public knowledge-base results are not exhaustive; verify official websites and business details independently. Search availability does not confirm a partner or payment status." }); } catch (_) { return json(request, env, { success: false, provider: "Wikidata", message: "Worldwide search is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later." }, 502); } } if (url.pathname === "/admin" && request.method === "GET") return new Response(adminPage(), { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8", "Cache-Control": "no-store", "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff", "X-Frame-Options": "DENY", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer", "Content-Security-Policy": "default-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; connect-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'", "Permissions-Policy": "camera=(), geolocation=()", "Strict-Transport-Security": "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains" } }); if (url.pathname === "/admin/payments" && request.method === "GET") return new Response(paymentAdminPage(), { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8", "Cache-Control": "no-store", "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff", "X-Frame-Options": "DENY", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer", "X-Robots-Tag": "noindex, nofollow", "Content-Security-Policy": "default-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; connect-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'", "Permissions-Policy": "camera=(), geolocation=()", "Strict-Transport-Security": "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains" } }); if (url.pathname === "/api/admin/payments" && request.method === "GET") { if (!env.ADMIN_TOKEN || request.headers.get("Authorization") !== `Bearer ${env.ADMIN_TOKEN}`) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Unauthorized. Check the administrator token." }, 401); if (!env.DB) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "D1 database is not configured." }, 503); const result = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT payment_id, reference, amount, currency, status, paypal_order_id, paypal_capture_id, mode, created_at, updated_at, paid_at FROM payments ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200").all(); const payments = (result.results || []).map(p => ({ id: p.payment_id, reference: p.reference, amount: p.amount, currency: p.currency, status: p.status, orderId: p.paypal_order_id, captureId: p.paypal_capture_id, mode: p.mode, createdAt: p.created_at, updatedAt: p.updated_at, paidAt: p.paid_at })); return json(request, env, { success: true, payments }); } if (url.pathname === "/api/requests" && request.method === "POST") { if (!env.DB) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Request storage is not configured yet." }, 503); const origin = request.headers.get("Origin") || ""; if (env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN && origin && origin !== env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Origin not allowed." }, 403); const parsedBody = await readJsonLimited(request, 20000); if (parsedBody.error) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? "Request is too large." : "Invalid request data." }, parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? 413 : 400); const body = parsedBody.value; const kind = safeString(body.kind, 30); const raw = body.fields && typeof body.fields === "object" && !Array.isArray(body.fields) ? body.fields : {}; // Honeypot field: bots that fill it are silently rejected as a generic error. if (safeString(raw.companyFax, 200)) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Could not submit this request. Please check the form and try again." }, 400); if (!ALLOWED_KINDS.has(kind)) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Unknown request type." }, 400); const fields = {}; for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(raw)) { if (!/^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,59}$/.test(k)) continue; fields[k] = safeString(v, k.toLowerCase().includes("message") || k.toLowerCase().includes("proposal") || k.toLowerCase().includes("details") ? 4000 : 500); } const requiredByKind = { business: ["requestType", "requestSector", "requestName", "requestEmail", "requestMessage", "requestConsent"], artist: ["artistType", "artistStageName", "artistLegalName", "artistEmail", "artistCountry", "artistProposal", "artistContactConsent"], consultation: ["consultationKind", "consultationCategory", "consultationName", "consultationEmail", "consultationDetails", "consultationConsent"], newsletter: ["email", "newsletterConsent"], suggestion: ["suggestionType", "suggestionMessage", "suggestionConsent"], "partner-proposal": ["companyName", "contactNameRole", "partnerEmail", "country", "proposalType", "sector", "proposalDetails", "termsAcknowledged", "dataConsent"], shipment: ["senderCompany", "senderEmail", "approvedReference", "contentsType", "quantityAndPackages", "purposeAndContents", "shipmentConsent"], "payment-notice": ["paymentMethod", "senderEmail", "paymentAmount", "paymentDate", "paymentReference", "paymentNoticeConsent"] }; } const consentFields = { business: ["requestConsent"], artist: ["artistContactConsent"], consultation: ["consultationConsent"], newsletter: ["newsletterConsent"], suggestion: ["suggestionConsent"], "partner-proposal": ["termsAcknowledged", "dataConsent"], shipment: ["shipmentConsent"], "payment-notice": ["paymentNoticeConsent"] }; for (const key of (consentFields[kind] || [])) { if (fields[key] !== "yes") return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Please confirm the required consent before submitting." }, 400); } const emailForValidation = fields.email || fields.requestEmail || fields.artistEmail || fields.consultationEmail || fields.partnerEmail || fields.senderEmail || fields.suggestionEmail || ""; const now = new Date().toISOString(); // Privacy-preserving per-IP throttle: store only a SHA-256 hash, never the raw IP. const requestIp = request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP") || "unknown"; const requestIpHash = await sha256Hex(requestIp); const requestWindow = new Date(Date.now() - 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); const requestAttempts = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM request_attempts WHERE ip_hash = ? AND created_at > ?").bind(requestIpHash, requestWindow).first(); if (Number(requestAttempts?.total || 0) >= 20) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Too many submissions from this connection. Please wait 10 minutes and try again." }, 429); const attemptSaved = await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO request_attempts (ip_hash, created_at) VALUES (?, ?)").bind(requestIpHash, now).run(); if (!attemptSaved.success) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Could not apply the request safety check. Please try again later." }, 500); // Basic duplicate throttle: same email and form type may submit once per 45 seconds. if (email) { const recent = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM requests WHERE email = ? AND kind = ? AND created_at > ? LIMIT 1") .bind(email, kind, new Date(Date.now() - 45000).toISOString()).first(); if (recent) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "A similar request was just received. Please wait a little before submitting again." }, 429); } const reference = makeReference(); const result = await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO requests (reference, kind, email, fields_json, status, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'new', ?)") .bind(reference, kind, email || null, JSON.stringify(fields), now).run(); if (!result.success) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "We could not save your request. Your entries have not been cleared; please try again later." }, 500); const notifySent = await notifyOwner(env, { reference, kind, fields, createdAt: now }); return json(request, env, { success: true, reference, stored: true, notificationSent: notifySent, message: "Request saved." }, 201); } if (url.pathname === "/api/admin/requests" && request.method === "GET") { if (!env.ADMIN_TOKEN || request.headers.get("Authorization") !== `Bearer ${env.ADMIN_TOKEN}`) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Unauthorized. Check the administrator token." }, 401); if (!env.DB) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "D1 database is not configured." }, 503); const status = url.searchParams.get("status") || "all"; let result; if (status === "all") result = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, reference, kind, email, fields_json, status, created_at FROM requests ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200").all(); else if (ALLOWED_STATUSES.has(status)) result = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, reference, kind, email, fields_json, status, created_at FROM requests WHERE status = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200").bind(status).all(); else return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Invalid status filter." }, 400); const requests = (result.results || []).map(r => ({ id: r.id, reference: r.reference, kind: r.kind, email: r.email, fields: JSON.parse(r.fields_json || "{}"), status: r.status, createdAt: r.created_at })); return json(request, env, { success: true, requests }); } const statusMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/api\/admin\/requests\/(\d+)\/status$/); if (statusMatch && request.method === "PATCH") { if (!env.ADMIN_TOKEN || request.headers.get("Authorization") !== `Bearer ${env.ADMIN_TOKEN}`) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Unauthorized." }, 401); const parsedBody = await readJsonLimited(request, 1000); if (parsedBody.error) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? "Request is too large." : "Invalid JSON." }, parsedBody.error === "too_large" ? 413 : 400); const body = parsedBody.value; const status = safeString(body.status, 30); if (!ALLOWED_STATUSES.has(status)) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Invalid request status." }, 400); const result = await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE requests SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(status, new Date().toISOString(), Number(statusMatch[1])).run(); if (!result.success || result.meta?.changes === 0) return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Request not found or status unchanged." }, 404); return json(request, env, { success: true, status }); } // Serve website assets from this same Worker origin after API routes. if (env.ASSETS && (request.method === "GET" || request.method === "HEAD")) { return env.ASSETS.fetch(request); } return json(request, env, { success: false, message: "Not found." }, 404); } }; /* D1 MIGRATION â€” run once in Cloudflare D1 SQL console; do not drop existing tables: -- Run this migration once in the existing Cloudflare D1 database used by Marrakechpixim. -- Do not drop or recreate the existing requests table. CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_ip_created ON payments(ip_hash, created_at); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_status_created ON payments(status, created_at); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_reference ON payments(reference);
